In registered studies where windows, populations, rules and binding budgets change from request to request, the framework's regret is below that of a trial with the same nominal rider-weeks and below dose interpolation of the same pilot data, and reusing its one-off preparation answers 60 requests 14.1x and 2.70x faster with identical answers. Against a nine-offer trial fitted with the framework's own dose curve, one-week regret is 0.055 lower.

</details>


### [146] [The Gold in Bias: Maturing the AI Design Process through Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29730)
*Samira Maghool,Paolo Ceravolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将AI偏见从"需最小化的缺陷"重新概念化为"诊断工具"，提出四维分析框架与层级证据框架，涵盖30种偏见类型、16种验证方法与20种对策，倡导全生命周期的"设计即伦理"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多将偏见视为孤立问题，而非可作为证据来强化AI全生命周期验证与治理的信号。偏见实际上反映了数据、建模假设与系统设计中的深层缺陷，因此有必要重新定位其为诊断工具。

Method: 提出多维框架，从四个维度分析偏见：来源、在AI建模生命周期中的出现点、技术与方法成因、检测与缓解的验证方法；构建横跨传统AI与生成式AI的偏见类型学，并提出区分内部效度与外部效度的层级证据框架，实现偏见类型—验证技术—对策的系统映射。

Result: 分析涵盖30种偏见类型、16种验证方法与20种对策，揭示偏见如何在各建模阶段显现与传播，并给出可供实践者使用的可操作路线图；层级证据框架阐明了不同验证策略对机制完整性与情境可靠性的贡献。

Conclusion: 倡导"设计即伦理"原则，将偏见验证贯穿开发生命周期，从而构建更公平、更稳健、更可信的AI系统。

Abstract: Bias in AI systems is typically framed as a flaw to be minimized, yet it also serves as a critical indicator of underlying weaknesses in data, modeling assumptions, and system design. Existing approaches often treat bias as an isolated problem rather than as evidence that can strengthen verification and governance across the AI lifecycle. This paper aims to reconceptualize bias as a diagnostic tool that supports rigorous AI verification. We seek to develop a multidimensional framework to analyze bias, demonstrate how biases emerge in both Traditional and Generative AI, and provide a structured pathway for verification-driven mitigation. We present a multidimensional framework analyzing bias across four dimensions: origin sources, emergence points throughout the AI modeling lifecycle, technical and methodological causes, and validation approaches for detection and mitigation. Through a comprehensive typology spanning traditional and generative AI systems, we demonstrate how biases manifest and propagate across development stages. Our analysis encompasses 30 distinct bias types, 16 verification methods, and 20 countermeasures, providing an actionable roadmap for practitioners. We introduce a hierarchical evidence framework that distinguishes internal validity (mechanistic integrity of AI systems) from external validity (contextual reliability in deployment environments). The framework reveals how biases manifest and propagate across modeling stages, enabling systematic mapping between bias types, verification techniques, and effective countermeasures. The proposed evidence hierarchy clarifies how different verification strategies contribute to mechanistic integrity and contextual reliability. We advocate for ''Ethics by Design'' principles that integrate bias verification throughout the development lifecycle, enabling the construction of fairer, more robust, and trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [147] [AI-based detection of worsening heart failure from low-resolution telemonitoring data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29742)
*Erik Aerts,Yinan Yu,Annika Rosengren,Michael Fu,Martin Lindgren,Falk Dippel,Martin Adiels,Helen Sjöland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACER模型（带对比事件表示的Transformer），预测心衰远程监测数据中通向住院事件的时间线，正确预测66.7%的时间线，过估7.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心衰面临高共病负担、患者老龄化和频繁住院的医疗挑战；远程监测可通过早期发现健康恶化来改善管理，且需要自主系统检测恶化迹象以减轻医护人员负担。

Method: TRACER模型：为每个生物标志物引入时间感知嵌入、通过表征学习增强异常检测的对比预训练、独立的二分类器；数据为276名心衰患者远程记录的生物标志物序列，按时间规则分成重叠窗口，并根据窗口后缘是否发生心衰相关住院进行标注。

Result: 在高度不平衡的真实数据集上正确预测66.7%的通向心衰住院的时间线，过估7.9%；将训练重构为事件检测问题比直接在预测窗口上训练表现更好，能更有效利用有限的住院事件。

Conclusion: TRACER在检测真实远程监测数据中的病情恶化迹象方面优于其他测试模型，有望生成警报以对心衰患者进行对抗性治疗。

Abstract: Objective: Heart failure (HF) presents a healthcare challenge due to its high comorbidity burden, aging patient population and frequent hospitalizations. Remote monitoring offers a promising approach to managing HF patients by early detection of health deterioration. Developing autonomous systems to detect signs of worsening in telemonitoring data is of interest to reduce the workload of healthcare personnel. Methods: We propose the TRACER model, a Transformer with Contrastive Event Representation, designed to predict timelines leading to rare hospitalization events in low-resolution and irregularly sampled telemonitoring data. TRACER incorporates time-aware embeddings for each biomarker, contrastive pre-training to enhance anomaly detection via representation learning, and independent binary classifiers for detection. We used measurement data containing remotely recorded biomarker sequences from 276 HF patients segmented into overlapping windows based on temporal rules, and labeled the windows based on the occurrence of HF relevant hospitalizations at the latter edge of the window. Results: TRACER was able to correctly predict 66.7% timelines leading up to HF hospitalizations in the highly imbalanced real-world dataset with an overestimation of 7.9%. Reformulating the training of TRACER as an event detection problem improved the predictive performance compared with training directly on forecasting windows, enabling more effective use of the limited hospitalization events. Conclusion: TRACER demonstrated superior performance in detecting signs of worsening status in real-world telemonitoring data compared to the other tested models. Significance: TRACER shows promise in identifying signs of clinical deterioration that allow for alerts to be generated to provide counteractive treatment in patients with HF.

</details>


### [148] [Breaking the Environment Wall: Evolving LLM Agent Environments for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29773)
*Yukai Wu,Yuanjing Yang,Le Zhou,Shaokun Han,Haoyu Wang,Zirui Tang,Weihuang Zheng,Maxm Pan,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Env-Rethink系统（27B后训练模型），通过构建收集地图与事件日志、识别环境噪声、生成虚拟事件历史演化环境，缓解非agent-ready环境带来的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实任务（如办公流程、科学实验）需LLM agent与环境反复交互，但环境往往非agent-ready：信息分散碎片化、相关证据混杂误导信息与冲突版本、环境随时间演化引入新噪声，导致SOTA agent性能大幅下降（如83.9%→57.6%）。

Method: Env-Rethink具备三项能力：(1) 自适应构建Collection Maps（组织相关文件）与Event Logs（上下文化跨数据关系）补充必要上下文；(2) 通过离线轨迹学习后训练模型，识别环境中的潜在噪声问题；(3) 通过虚拟事件历史改变环境状态与证据关系，演化出更棘手的环境以促进agent进一步改进。

Result: 实验显示Env-Rethink能有效提升下游任务表现：在30个任务、9个模型上，rubric通过率提升超过15.1%。

Conclusion: Env-Rethink通过补充上下文、识别噪声与演化环境三方面能力，可有效提升LLM agent在复杂非结构化环境中的任务表现。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks (e.g., office workflows, scientific experimentation) require LLM agents to interact repeatedly with their environments for context-dependent operations. However, such environments are often not agent-ready. First, information is often scattered and fragmented across the environment. Second, relevant evidence in the environment is often mixed with misleading information and conflicting versions. Third, environments evolve over time, introducing new noise and more challenging tasks. These challenges can substantially degrade performance for state-of-the-art AI agents (e.g., from 83.9% to 57.6%). To address these challenges, we propose Env-Rethink (a system with 27B post-trained model) that supports three main capabilities: (1) It adaptively builds Collection Maps (for organizing related files) and Event Logs (for contextualizing cross-data relationships) to supplement necessary context; (2) It further leverages the post-trained model (through offline trajectory learning) to identify underlying noise issues in the environment; (3) It ultimately evolves environments through virtual event histories that alter environmental states and evidence relationships, producing more tricky ones for further agent improvement. Experiments show that Env-Rethink can effectively improve downstream task performance (with over 15.1% rubric pass rate improvement across nine models on 30 tasks).

</details>


### [149] [Hallucination Neurons and Where to Find Them: An Investigation into the existence of Hallucination Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29781)
*Huseyin Cavus,Sebin Sabu,Joshua Spear,Jaskaran Singh Kawatra,Pavithra Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个五步诊断协议，用于检验稀疏探针方法对LLM神经元定位主张的有效性。通过对H-neurons在多个开源LLM和数据集上的复现，发现检测效果可复制，但所选神经元并不具有唯一局部性，表明稀疏预测结构可能与神经元选择的非唯一性共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释机器学习中，稀疏探针方法常被用于识别能检测并因果影响LLM行为（如事实回忆、安全对齐、幻觉）的小规模神经元集合，但这些主张很少在相关高维特征空间中针对L1正则化探针的已知失效模式进行检验。这对模型审计和行为引导有重要影响，因此需要更严格的验证标准。

Method: 提出五步诊断协议：特征相关性、自助法稳定性、稀疏与稠密排序分歧、干预基线、跨数据集评估。将该协议应用于H-neurons，使用开源LLM在TriviaQA、BioASQ和NQ-Open数据集上进行研究，并在n=500、五个随机种子下进行因果验证。

Result: 检测结果在两个模型和数据集上均可复现，并在TriviaQA和BioASQ上超过原始报告的AUROC差距。Gemma 3 4B在匹配数据集上持续优于MedGemma 4B（TriviaQA +0.311 vs +0.235，BioASQ +0.474 vs +0.455，NQ-Open +0.128 vs +0.112）。因果验证显示效应显著超过随机同层基线。但诊断结果表明所选神经元并不唯一局部化：Gemma 3 4B三种设置中22个H-Neurons有19个的Pearson |r| > 0.7，自助法选择稳定性中等，稀疏与稠密排序重叠较弱。

Conclusion: 稀疏预测结构可以与神经元选择的非唯一性共存。在机制可解释性中，常规诊断验证对于区分“检测主张”和“定位主张”是必要的。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning for Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly relies on sparse probing methods that identify small sets of neurons claimed to detect and causally influence behaviors such as factuality recall, safety alignment, and hallucination. These claims have important implications for model auditing and behavioral steering, yet they are rarely tested against known failure modes of $L_1$-regularized probing in correlated, high-dimensional feature spaces. We propose a five-step diagnostic protocol covering feature correlation, bootstrap stability, sparse versus dense ranking disagreement, intervention baselines, and cross-dataset evaluation as a minimum standard for sparse-neuron localization claims. We investigate prior work using our proposed approach, specifically on H-neurons using open-source LLMs across TriviaQA, BioASQ, and NQ-Open datasets. Our results demonstrate detection replicates across both models and datasets, and exceeds the original reported AUROC gaps for TriviaQA and BioASQ datasets. Gemma 3 4B consistently outperforms MedGemma 4B on matched datasets, with AUROC gaps of +0.311 versus +0.235 on TriviaQA, +0.474 versus +0.455 on BioASQ, and +0.128 versus +0.112 on NQ-Open respectively. Causal validation at $n = 500$ with five random seeds shows statistically significant effects beyond random same-layer baselines. At the same time, the diagnostic results indicate that the selected neurons are not uniquely localized. Across the three Gemma 3 4B settings, 19 of 22 selected H-Neurons have Pearson $|r| > 0.7$ with other features, bootstrap selections show only moderate stability, and sparse and dense rankings overlap only weakly. Our findings show that sparse predictive structure can coexist with non-unique neuron selection. Routine diagnostic validation is necessary to distinguish detection claims from localization claims in mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [150] [Learning to Ideate for Scientific Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29802)
*Shubham Kale,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究能否用延迟的引用影响信号作为反馈来引导科学创意生成，构建10万+计算机论文数据集训练目标条件奖励模型，并用SFT+RL对齐生成器，实验证明RL调优模型能产出更高估计影响力的想法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学创意系统多以新颖性、清晰性、可行性等即时可评判的代理指标进行训练和评估，无法回答延迟的科学采纳信号能否作为反馈将模型引向更高预期影响力研究方向的问题。

Method: 从10万+计算机科学论文中抽取目标条件下的想法描述，为每篇论文赋予序数、年份归一化的引用标签；训练目标条件奖励模型从研究目标与想法对预测引用影响；用该奖励经监督微调与强化学习对齐想法生成器；采用留出、基于参考的评估协议，在同一目标下将生成想法与历史想法比较并按参考想法影响力加权，以降低循环性。

Result: RL调优模型产出的想法在估计影响力上持续高于基座模型和监督微调基线。

Conclusion: 科学影响力可作为实用的、结果导向的反馈信号，用于对齐LLM进行开放式科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific ideation is increasingly mediated by large language models, but current ideation systems are usually trained and evaluated on immediately judgeable proxies such as novelty, clarity, and feasibility. This leaves open whether delayed signals of scientific uptake can be used as feedback for steering models toward research directions with higher expected \emph{impact}. We study this question using citation-normalized impact as a noisy but scalable proxy for scholarly uptake. We construct a large-scale dataset from over 100K computer science papers by extracting goal-conditioned idea descriptions and assigning each paper an ordinal, year-normalized citation label. We then train a goal-conditioned reward model to predict citation-impact labels from research goal and idea pairs, and use this reward to align an idea generator through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. To reduce circularity, we evaluate generated ideas with a held-out, reference-grounded protocol that compares model outputs against historical ideas under the same research goal and weights judgments by the reference idea's citation-impact label. Experiments show that our RL-tuned model consistently produces ideas with higher estimated impact than both the base model and supervised fine-tuning baselines. Our findings position scientific impact as a practical, outcome-grounded feedback signal for aligning LLMs in open-ended scientific discovery.

</details>


### [151] [Decoding Imagined Speech: A Strictly Subject-Independent Approach Using EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29820)
*Frederik Møllskov Trier,Xiaopeng Mao,Sadasivan Puthusserypady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对想象语音EEG数据集进行严格的跨被试（subject-independent）评测，比较时域统计特征与频域谱带功率两种管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象语音EEG解码备受关注，但已有报告的性能常依赖不能清晰反映跨被试泛化的评测协议，缺乏透明基线。

Method: 采用严格subject-independent框架；比较时域统计特征与频域谱带功率两种预处理/特征提取管线；使用被试级交叉验证、试次级多数投票与随机森林分类器；并进行前向特征选择。

Result: 谱管线平均试次准确率显著高于统计管线（49.03±4.18% vs 37.97±3.79%）；前向特征选择显示少数频带即可捕获大部分判别信息。

Conclusion: 该工作为面向跨被试泛化改进的EEG想象语音BCI研究提供了坚实基线。

Abstract: Imagined speech decoding from electroencephalography (EEG) has gained increasing attention as a potential communication pathway for individuals with severe motor impairments, yet reported performance often relies on evaluation protocols that do not clearly reflect cross-subject generalization. This study presents a transparent baseline investigation of a multi-class imagined speech EEG dataset under a strictly subject-independent evaluation framework. Two preprocessing and feature extraction pipelines were compared: a time-domain statistical feature approach and a frequency-domain spectral bandpower approach, evaluated using subject-wise cross-validation and trial-level majority voting with a random forest classifier. The spectral pipeline achieved a significantly higher mean trial-wise accuracy than the statistical pipeline (49.03 $\pm$ 4.18% vs. 37.97 $\pm$ 3.79%) for coarse-level classification across subjects. Forward feature selection further indicated that a limited subset of frequency bands captured most of the discriminative information. Overall, this work provides a strong basis for future brain-computer interface studies targeting improved cross-subject generalization in EEG-based imagined speech decoding.

</details>


### [152] [A Risk-Adaptive and Evidence-Constrained Framework for Generative AI Feedback in Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29874)
*Shihao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个风险自适应、证据约束的入门编程反馈框架，用校准风险定干预时机、用记录证据约束反馈内容，实现从自查到局部提示的渐进式帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI可将学习分析转为个性化支持，但反馈系统须决定何时干预、用哪些证据、给多少帮助。

Method: 基于215名学生2993个失败提交状态，训练学生不交叉的持续失败预测模型；为136个案例生成4种匹配反馈条件；用校准风险指导容量受限干预策略；经标准化修复与证据门控生成消息。

Result: 验证选出的逻辑回归测试PR-AUC为0.550、ROC-AUC为0.681；更完整学生历史提升未修改重提交预测；修复与门控后544条新消息中519条含全部必需组件；固定阈值序贯策略选中17.8%合格测试状态，捕获25.2%观察到的持续失败。

Conclusion: 支持证据门控的渐进式帮助策略：校准风险引导干预时机，记录证据约束反馈内容，必要时从自查推进到局部提示；框架连接预测、决策与有依据生成，并保持各自评估结果独立。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence can turn learning analytics into personalized support, but feedback systems must decide when to intervene, which evidence to use, and how much assistance to provide. We developed a risk-adaptive, evidence-constrained framework for introductory programming using 2993 failed-submission states from 215 students. Student-disjoint models predicted persistent failure and related outcomes; four matched feedback conditions were generated for 136 cases; and calibrated risk informed capacity-limited intervention policies. The validation-selected logistic regression model achieved a test precision-recall area under the curve of 0.550 and a receiver operating characteristic area under the curve of 0.681. Broader student histories improved prediction of unmodified resubmission. After standardized repair and evidence gating, 519 of 544 newly generated messages contained all required components. A fixed-threshold sequential policy selected 17.8% of eligible test states and captured 25.2% of observed persistent failures. These findings support an evidence-gated progressive assistance strategy: calibrated risk guides intervention timing, recorded evidence constrains feedback content, and assistance progresses from self-checks to localized hints when warranted. The framework connects prediction, decision-making, and grounded generation while keeping their evaluation outcomes distinct.

</details>


### [153] [Ontology-Mediated Neurosymbolic Constraint Acquisition from Multiple Stakeholders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29876)
*Stefan Bischof,Juliana Kainz,Danilo Valerio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种架构，用OWL配置本体在神经约束源与下游消费者间进行中介，弥补神经符号研究中上游需求获取与形式化的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号研究通常假设已有符号规范，忽略了上游需求与约束的获取和形式化难题。

Method: 用OWL配置本体统一异构输入：LLM助手获取软性利益相关者偏好，硬件规范定义硬性物理工程限制；借助描述逻辑检测不可满足性，生成符号解释让LLM与用户交互式重新协商条件，剩余冲突由下游基于优先级的松弛解决。

Result: 在FLEXI项目的微电网用例上进行了演示。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至约束获取分散于人类与自动化来源、且权威不等的多利益相关者领域。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic research typically assumes a pre-existing symbolic specification, leaving the upstream challenge of acquiring and formalizing requirements and constraints largely unaddressed. We present an architecture that fills this gap by using an OWL configuration ontology to mediate between neural constraint sources and downstream consumers. In this framework, LLM assistants elicit soft stakeholder preferences, while hardware specifications define hard physical and engineering limits. The ontology unifies these heterogeneous inputs, leverages description logic to identify unsatisfiability, and generates symbolic explanations that enable LLMs to interactively renegotiate terms with users. Any remaining conflicts are resolved downstream via priority-based relaxation. We illustrate our approach on a microgrid use case from the FLEXI project and argue its generalizability to multi-stakeholder domains where constraint acquisition is distributed across human and automated sources of unequal authority.

</details>


### [154] [Who Holds the Pen? Let Specifications, Not Agents, Sign Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29921)
*Haiqing Li,Xin Ma,Yinhao Wu,Wenliang Zhong,Feng Jiang,Thao M. Dang,Xiao Hu,Hehuan Ma,Yuzhi Guo,Junzhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有LLM智能体将任务规范仅作为上下文，导致"理解-执行"和"状态-权威"两个缺口；论文提出将智能体提议与权威状态分离，并通过SpecHarness将规范编译为可追踪义务来治理执行与完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在单一循环中同时进行生成、决策、执行与自我评估，外部规范（指令、指南、输出模式、技能）仅作为同一模型的上下文，缺乏独立的规范权威边界，导致理解-执行缺口和状态-权威缺口。

Method: 在SkillsBench上仅使用智能体可见的提示、工作区信息和注入技能规范，提取509个有源依据的任务方向；提出将智能体提议与权威状态分离的原则，并实现SpecHarness：将可见规范编译为源链接义务，通过版本化义务状态治理执行与最终化，可验证需求在运行时被中介或验证，模糊主观需求保持建议性质。

Result: 七种模型仅有79.6%–86.4%的任务被满足，而完成声明率比官方评估通过率高28.7–37.9个百分点；在指南遵循和工件生成任务上，规范可作为合规执行与完成的权威。

Conclusion: 规范不应仅是行为指导，而应成为对合规执行与完成的权威；通过分离提议与权威状态、以义务版本状态治理执行，可弥合理解-执行与状态-权威缺口。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly combine generation, decision-making, execution, and self-evaluation within a single agentic loop. Although they operate under external specifications such as task instructions, guidelines, output schemas, and reusable skills, these specifications typically remain context for the same model that acts and declares completion, leaving no independent specification authority boundary. We identify two resulting gaps. The understanding--execution gap arises when a requirement is understood but not satisfied in execution; the state--authority gap arises when an agent's interpretation or completion claim does not establish the required state. On SkillsBench, using only agent-visible prompts, workspace information, and injected skill specifications, we extract 509 source-grounded task directions. Across seven models, only 79.6%--86.4% are satisfied, while completion-claim rates exceed official evaluator pass rates by 28.7--37.9 percentage points. We therefore separate agent proposals from authoritative state. Agents may plan, act, and request completion, but only admissible evidence from qualified providers may establish specification-governed state. SpecHarness operationalizes this principle by compiling visible specifications into source-linked obligations and governing execution and finalization through versioned obligation state. Verifiable requirements are mediated or validated at runtime, while ambiguous or subjective requirements remain advisory. Experiments on guideline-following and artifact-generation tasks show that specifications can serve not merely as behavioral guidance, but as authority over compliant execution and completion.

</details>


### [155] [Neuro-symbolic AI for Industrial Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29947)
*Danilo Valerio,Philipp Kogler,Stefan Bischof,Thomas Hubauer,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张用神经符号(NeSy)AI构建工业级配置器，提出三种NeSy集成策略的分类法，并报告其在工业配置copilot中的落地实践与设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的随机性使其单独难以胜任工业产品配置，因为输出必须语法有效、与包含数百特征和规则的知识库语义一致，并可由现有制造链生产。需要可靠、可解释、可信的配置器。

Method: 提出NeSy集成策略分类法，包含三类：混合推理(hybrid inference)、混合微调(hybrid fine-tuning)、混合训练(hybrid training)，并探讨其在配置领域的应用；在工业配置copilot中实践NeSy概念。

Result: 在工业配置copilot中操作化了NeSy概念，并总结出一套在工程环境中部署可信AI的实用设计选择。

Conclusion: 讨论了最紧迫的开放研究挑战，特别是如何将NeSy方法从学术小型演示扩展到工业级配置器的规模。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive performance on a wide range of generative tasks. Yet their probabilistic nature makes them, in isolation, fundamentally unsuited for industrial product configuration, where outputs must be syntactically valid, semantically consistent with a knowledge base of hundreds of features and rules, and producible by an existing manufacturing chain. We argue that Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI methods lay out a promising path towards industrial-grade configurators that are reliable by design, explainable, and trustworthy. This paper describes a taxonomy of three NeSy integration strategies, namely hybrid inference, hybrid fine-tuning, and hybrid training, exploring their usage in the configuration domain. We report our effort to operationalize NeSy concepts in an industrial configuration copilot and derive a set of practical design choices for deploying trustworthy AI in engineering environments. We close with a discussion of open research challenges we consider most pressing, in particular how to scale NeSy methods from small academic demonstrators to the size of industrial configurators.

</details>


### [156] [ENDOPROMPT: Victim-Side Pseudo-References for Utility Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29948)
*Qingyu Wu,Zeyu Feng,Yongda Yu,Yuzhe Luo,Hua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ENDOPROMPT，一种白盒方法，从无标注指令中学习降低效用的前缀，导致指令微调模型平均效用下降26.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入可在不产生有害内容的情况下降低良性任务表现，但许多攻击目标依赖任务标签或预设的目标响应，限制了攻击的通用性。

Method: 生成器以请求文本为输入；用干净受害者续写作为伪参考，通过局部搜索找到降低续写似然的前缀，再经同一指令内比较的偏好拟合与奖励精炼，将信号蒸馏进生成器。部署时每个请求生成一个前缀，无需受害者端搜索。

Result: 在四个指令微调模型和七个良性基准的完整划分上，平均效用变化为-26.8个百分点，28个单元格中27个为负。失败分析显示输出膨胀和前缀复用；对照实验未证明请求匹配带来退化优势。

Conclusion: 受害者派生监督无需基准反馈或预设失败响应，即可揭示效用弱点。代码将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Prompt injection can degrade benign task performance without eliciting harmful content. Yet many attack objectives depend on task labels or predefined target responses. We present ENDOPROMPT, a white-box method that learns utility-degrading prefixes from unlabeled instructions. Its generator takes the request text as input. Clean victim continuations serve as pseudo-references: local search identifies prefixes that reduce continuation likelihood, and preference fitting on comparisons within the same instruction, followed by reward refinement, distills this signal into a generator. At deployment, the generator produces one prefix per request without further victim-side search. Across four instruction-tuned models and the complete splits of seven benign benchmarks, ENDOPROMPT yields a mean utility change of -26.8 percentage points; 27 of 28 cells are negative. Failure analysis reveals output expansion and prefix reuse; the controls do not establish a degradation advantage from request matching. Victim-derived supervision can reveal utility weaknesses without benchmark feedback or prescribed failure responses. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [157] [Synthetic Hospital: An Open, Verifiable, Physician-Validated Longitudinal EHR Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30027)
*Christine Park,Valerie Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Synthetic Hospital——一个开放、完全合成、事实有据的纵向电子健康记录基准，用于评测临床AI。它基于公开医学教育资料构建，包含1,268名患者和5,602次就诊，所有诊断、发现和时序关系均锚定于标准本体（ICD-10-CM、SNOMED CT、LOINC）并有完整溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型很少用于临床工作流，原因是缺乏真实、纵向的基准：真实EHR因隐私、伦理和数据使用问题无法公开共享，且缺乏可验证的ground truth（病历只记录临床医生所记录的内容）。

Method: 完全基于公开医学教育资料构建，无受保护健康信息；提供模拟医院记录系统，镜像真实EHR基础设施（标准互操作API、基于角色的访问、函数调用接口）；通过双盲医生评审与10个前沿及开源模型评测验证其真实性与难度。

Result: 医生区分其记录与真实病历的准确率接近随机（53%）；10个模型中无一接近上限：最佳模型重建患者纵向问题列表的严重度加权F1为0.73，与匹配子集上7位医生的均值持平，但远低于最佳医生（0.89），且总结病历时漏掉约一半临床相关发现。

Conclusion: Synthetic Hospital 是一个困难且真实的临床AI性能测试，能够同时解决开放共享与可验证ground truth两大障碍。

Abstract: Frontier language models are rarely used in clinical workflows because the realistic, longitudinal benchmarks needed to develop them are scarce. Real electronic health record (EHR) data cannot be openly shared due to privacy, ethics or data use issues and it does not contain verifiable ground truth since the chart records only reflect what clinicians documented. We introduce Synthetic Hospital, an open, fully synthetic, fact-grounded longitudinal EHR benchmark that resolves the open sharing and verifiable ground truth barriers. Built entirely from public medical-education material with no protected health information, it comprises 1,268 longitudinal patients and 5,602 encounters, where every diagnosis, finding, and temporal relation is grounded in standard ontologies (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) and with a complete provenance chain back to its source medical education material. Synthetic Hospital is served through a simulated hospital record system that mirrors real EHR infrastructure (standard interoperability APIs, role-based access and function-calling interface). In a blinded review, physicians distinguished its records from real patient charts at near-chance rates (53\%). Across 10 frontier and open models, none approaches ceiling: the best model reconstructs a patient's longitudinal problem list with a severity-weighted F1 of 0.73, level with the mean of seven physicians on a matched subset but well below the best of them (0.89), and misses roughly half of clinically relevant findings when summarizing a chart. Overall, these results highlight that Synthetic Hospital is a difficult and realistic test of clinical AI performance.

</details>


### [158] [How does Adversarial Influence Scale in Multi-Agent Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30028)
*Addison J. Wu,Jasin Cekinmez,Michel Liao,Karthik Narasimhan,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体审议能提升性能，但当部分智能体不诚实（欺骗）时，群体规模扩大并非有效防御，关键在于欺骗者比例而非绝对数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实践中智能体可能出于自身目标或外部指令进行欺骗、破坏群体决策，需了解随着群体规模增大和欺骗者增多，群体对欺骗的易感性如何变化。

Method: 通过多智能体审议实验，操纵群体规模与欺骗者比例，测量“倒戈率”（初始正确答案的智能体转向错误最终答案的频率），并与人类从众研究进行对比；同时考察不同模型组合及欺骗者私下协调的影响。

Result: 倒戈率随欺骗者比例线性上升，而非取决于群体绝对人数；与人类仅在误导者占多数时才被影响不同，LLM智能体即使在欺骗者占少数时也经常倒戈；易感性取决于交互的模型（尤其诚实方模型）；允许欺骗者私下协调反而可能降低其效果。

Conclusion: 增加更多智能体不足以作为防御手段，因为对手可以随群体规模一起扩展；防御应关注欺骗者比例而非单纯扩大群体。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation can improve performance, but what happens when some agents do not act in good faith? In practice, an agent may be deceptive and work to subvert the group, whether through its own objectives or external instruction. We study how susceptibility to deception scales as groups increase in size and deceivers become more prevalent. It is not the number of agents in the group that matters, but the proportion of deceivers. We observe that the defection rate, how often initially correct agents switch to an incorrect final answer, rises linearly with this proportion. Whereas humans in comparable conformity studies are reliably swayed only when misleading confederates form a majority, LLM agents defect regularly even when deceivers remain a minority. Susceptibility also depends on which models are interacting, especially on the honest agent side. Unexpectedly, allowing deceivers to coordinate privately can make them less effective. Altogether, our results show that adding more agents is therefore not a sufficient defense, because the adversary can simply scale with the group.

</details>


### [159] [NNV3: Expanding Neural Network Verification to New Architectures and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30050)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Samuel Sasaki,Ben Wooding,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Muhammad Usama Zubair,Navid Hashemi,Hongchao Zhang,Waseem Abbas,Ipek Oguz,Meiyi Ma,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NNV3 是 MATLAB 深度学习与学习使能CPS形式化验证框架 NNV 的最新版本，通过扩展 Star-set 家族并引入概率与公平性验证，成为覆盖多类AI系统的综合验证工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NNV 1.0/2.0 已支持基础网络与部分动态模型，但面对权重扰动、视频/3D体数据、图神经网络、确定性验证不可行问题及公平性属性等新需求时能力不足，需要更通用、更易用的验证框架。

Method: 在 NNV 1.0（FFNN、CNN、NNCS）与 2.0（RNN、SSNN、neural ODE）的集合式可达性基础上，NNV3 引入 Star-set 新成员：ModelStar（权重扰动）、VolumeStar（视频与3D体输入）、GraphStar（图神经网络）；并加入基于共形推断的概率可达模式与 FairNNV（反事实与个体公平性验证），同时提供新基准与统一文档站点与教程。

Result: NNV3 支持更多模型与输入类型，新增恶意软件检测、图电力系统、医学影像、变长时序、动作识别等基准；概率可达模式补充了确定性验证不可行场景下的分析能力；FairNNV 可在连续输入区域上认证公平性属性。

Conclusion: NNV3 使 NNV 走向成熟，成为面向多样化 AI 系统的综合、鲁棒且易用的验证工具。

Abstract: We present NNV3, the latest version of the Neural Network Verification (NNV) tool, a MATLAB framework for formal verification of deep learning models and learning-enabled cyber-physical systems. Building on the set-based reachability foundation of NNV 1.0 (FFNNs, CNNs, NNCS) and NNV 2.0 (RNNs, SSNNs, neural ODEs), NNV3 introduces new members of the Star-set family: ModelStar for verifying networks under weight perturbation, VolumeStar for video and 3D volumetric inputs, and GraphStar for graph neural networks. A conformal-inference-based probabilistic reachability mode complements sound analysis for problems where deterministic verification is intractable, while FairNNV certifies counterfactual and individual fairness properties over continuous input regions. NNV3 introduces new benchmarks for malware detection, graph-based power-system models, medical imaging, variable-length time series data, and action recognition. NNV3 also incorporates tutorials and developer guides through a unified documentation site. This paper details these major updates, demonstrating NNV's maturation into a comprehensive, robust, and accessible verification tool for a diverse range of AI systems.

</details>


### [160] [SciWalker: Synthesizing Scientific Coding Problems with Operator Graphs and Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30054)
*Chenxi Li,Wenxuan Zeng,Yun Luo,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Yu Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciWalker通过算子链采样与执行反馈合成科学编程问题，构建8178道覆盖5大领域的高质量题目，用于强化学习提升LLM科学编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM科学编码能力提升依赖高质量训练数据，但人工编写真实问题成本高、耗时长，且难以系统覆盖多样科学领域与算法组合，导致此类数据稀缺。

Method: 框架将科学库接口与操作模式结合实例化算子，组织为算子图并采样算子链作为计算工作流线索；以此引导LLM生成有科学依据的问题陈述、参考解与测试，失败生成通过执行反馈迭代修复，并结合结构化工作流组合与验证、质量审查。

Result: 构建8178道高质量问题，覆盖5个科学领域、32个子领域；在Qwen3.5-9B上用GSPO算法做强化学习，SciCode子问题准确率从29.3%提升至39.2%（+9.9个百分点），并在科学代码生成、代码修复与推理基准上均有增益。

Conclusion: SciWalker实现可扩展、科学有据、多样且可执行的科学编程任务生成，有效提升LLM科学编码与推理能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Improving the scientific coding capabilities of large language models (LLMs) requires high-quality training data. However, such data remain scarce because manually authoring realistic problems is costly and time-consuming, while systematically covering diverse scientific domains and algorithmic combinations remains challenging. To address this, we introduce SciWalker, a framework for synthesizing scientific coding problems through operator-chain sampling and execution feedback. The framework combines scientific library interfaces with operation modes to instantiate operators, organizes them into operator graphs, and samples operator chains as computational workflow cues. Guided by these cues, we adopt LLMs to generate scientifically grounded problem statements, reference solutions, and tests, with failed generations iteratively repaired using execution feedback. By combining structured workflow composition with verification and quality review, SciWalker enables scalable task generation while promoting scientific grounding, computational diversity, and executability. Using this framework, we construct 8,178 high-quality problems spanning 5 scientific domains and 32 subdomains. To evaluate their training utility, we conduct reinforcement learning on Qwen3.5-9B using the GSPO algorithm. This training improves SciCode subproblem accuracy by 9.9 percentage points, from 29.3% to 39.2%, with gains across scientific code generation, code repair, and reasoning benchmarks. The code for SciWalker is available at https://github.com/lichenx1/SciWalker.

</details>


### [161] [Screen Before You Serve: Simulation for Production Customer Experience AI Agents at 140M Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30137)
*Edesio Alcoba,Kevin Rossell,Aman Gupta,Shao Tang,Jiwoo Hong,Pabel Carrillo-Mendoza,Wanderson Conceição Ferreira,Alvaro Tedeschi,Zayd Simjee,Shreya Rajpal,Bruno Finardi Hime,Christian Sousa,Luis Moneda,Herbert Fei,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于假设驱动的模拟工作流，用于在部署前筛选客服（CX）智能体，并在 Nubank 高流量客服智能体上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CX智能体需检测意图、遵循复杂运营政策并可靠调用工具，但在受监管行业中难以改进：人工端到端测试覆盖有限，而线上实验会让客户暴露于失败风险并损害信任。

Method: 采用假设驱动的模拟工作流：合成客户对智能体响应做出反应，模拟工具输出支持多步智能体流程而不调用生产后端；使用 Snowglobe 模拟器在 Nubank 的 Card Delivery 智能体及其后继 Card Management 上进行评估与迭代。

Result: 在4个部署版本上，模拟与生产版本的二元评估分数高度相关；模拟引导迭代使真实 A/B 测试中 tNPS 提升 36.69 分；在超16,000次模拟对话中筛选开放权重配置，后续 A/B 测试中选定模型将自助服务率（SSR）提升 8.82 个百分点至 Nubank 历史最高，tNPS 无显著变化。

Conclusion: 模拟使模型、推理设置和提示词的广泛探索无需让客户暴露于风险，实现了仅靠线上实验难以实现的生产改进。

Abstract: Customer experience (CX) agents use tools and large language models to address customer requests and guide conversational interactions with an organization's products. Improving these agents, especially in regulated industries, is difficult: they must detect intent, follow complex operational policies and use tools reliably. Manual end-to-end testing offers limited coverage, while live experiments expose customers to failures that can erode trust.
  We present a hypothesis-driven simulation workflow for screening candidate CX agents before deployment. Synthetic customers react to agent responses and simulated tool outputs enable multi-step agentic workflows without invoking production backends. We use the Snowglobe simulator on Nubank's Card Delivery agent and its expanded successor, Card Management - Nubank's highest-volume chat-support agent in Brazil. Across 4 deployed versions, simulated and production version-level binary evaluator scores show high correlation. Simulation-guided iteration increased transactional net promoter score (tNPS) by 36.69 points in a live A/B test. We also screened open-weight configurations in over 16,000 simulated conversations. In a subsequent live A/B test, the selected model increased self-service rate (SSR) by 8.82 percentage points to the highest level observed at Nubank, with no statistically significant change in tNPS. Simulation made broad exploration of models, reasoning settings, and prompts feasible without customer exposure, enabling production improvements that would have been impractical to pursue through live experimentation alone.

</details>


### [162] [EnigmaForge: The Question Is Hidden in the Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30144)
*Daniel Eisner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnigmaForge是一个无需提问、仅给旧文档堆的基准，模型需从信件、收据等中挖掘唯一可解的逻辑谜题；用SAT求解器保证唯一解且每条线索必不可少，语料可无限生成。25个前沿模型在600+实例上测试，发现"直觉"指标重塑排行榜（22倍差距），且内容过滤器会干扰评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常直接向模型提问。作者想测试一种更贴近真实、更难的场景：模型只拿到一堆无问题的旧文档，需要自己发现并解决其中隐藏的逻辑谜题，以衡量模型的"直觉"能力。

Method: 将逻辑谜题埋入信件、收据、日志等文档中，生成时用SAT求解器证明解唯一，并提供消融证书证明每条线索都不可或缺。实例为程序生成，可无限更新语料。设定三组匹配条件，用"任务成功"作为主指标衡量直觉，"世界重建"作为次轴。

Result: 25个前沿模型在600+实例、17,400条评分记录上评测。直觉指标导致排行榜大洗牌：直觉差距达22倍，而事实检索能力仅1.6倍；事实检索第二名在直觉上排第十四；一个模型被告知问题与否表现无差别，另一个模型不被提问时反而显著更好。多个模型在触及谜题前被自身内容过滤器拦截。

Conclusion: 仅提供文档、不提问的设定能揭示模型间"直觉"能力的巨大差异，与事实检索能力并不一致。此外，任何将拒答计为失败的基准实际上可能在测量内容过滤行为，而非模型真实能力。

Abstract: Most benchmarks hand the model a question. EnigmaForge hands it a stack of old documents and no question at all. Buried in the letters, receipts, and logbook margins is a small logic puzzle whose solution is unique - proved by a SAT solver at generation time, with an ablation certificate showing every clue is load-bearing. Because instances are generated rather than collected, the corpus renews forever. The headline measure is intuition: task success when handed only the story, with world reconstruction as the secondary axis. Twenty-five frontier models ran over 600 instances (17,400 scored records) under three matched conditions. Intuition reshuffles the leaderboard: a 22x spread where fact recovery spans 1.6x, the second-best fact-recoverer ranks fourteenth, one model is indifferent to being told the question, and another is significantly better without it. Several models were blocked by their own content filters before reaching the puzzle - any benchmark scoring refusals as failure is quietly measuring filter behavior.

</details>


### [163] [Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30177)
*Nayoung Choi,Shengjian Chen,Xiaokai Wei,Wenzheng Zhang,Daiyao Yi,Rachit Pareek,Vincent Su,Michelle Gong,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种搜索感知的强化学习框架用于查询理解(QU)，采用"先蒸馏后RL"范式：先通过师生SFT获得合规的策略初始化，再用基于搜索引擎实时交互、针对各组件角色的奖励来优化各QU组件。在Roblox搜索上的实验表明，组件特定优化同时提升了各组件效用和下游搜索质量，NDCG@20较SFT策略提升8.9分，较单一端到端奖励训练提升3.5分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询理解在生产搜索系统中至关重要，负责将原始用户查询转化为检索执行计划，驱动下游检索与排序。LLM虽使QU可被建模为结构化多任务生成问题（如意图分类、查询扩展），但优化这类模型以产生与搜索引擎耦合的输出仍具挑战：静态的、基于标签的监督无法捕获各组件如何与底层搜索管线交互并影响下游性能。

Method: 提出面向QU的搜索感知强化学习框架，基于"先蒸馏后RL"的范式。第一阶段用师生监督微调(SFT)得到格式良好、符合schema的策略初始化；第二阶段RL用来源于与搜索引擎实时交互的奖励来优化各QU组件，奖励按各组件在其操作角色上的贡献量身定制，而非用与最终搜索结果绑定的单一奖励。

Result: 在Roblox搜索上的实验显示，组件特定的优化同时提升了各组件效用和下游搜索质量：NDCG@20较SFT策略提升8.9个点，较使用单一端到端奖励的训练提升3.5个点。

Conclusion: 将QU优化解耦为按组件、与搜索引擎实时交互的强化学习奖励设计，能有效超越静态监督和单一端到端奖励，在提升各组件效用的同时带来显著的下游搜索质量增益。

Abstract: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.

</details>


### [164] [SAGE: Mitigating Long-Horizon Reasoning Biases via Topological Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30192)
*Xinyue Zeng,Jiawei Zhang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE框架通过代数稀疏化与双曲结构引导两种结构先验，缓解长程推理中的探索偏置与复合偏置，在12个基准、7个模型族上超越基线，Andrews-Curtis任务提升达8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在稀疏奖励下长程推理脆弱，根源是复杂推理空间诱导的两种偏置：探索偏置（偏向局部合理但不稳定的分支）与复合偏置（局部偏差随深度累积抑制稀有奖励）。

Method: 提出Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA)理论视角，刻画分支结构与稀疏奖励如何诱导上述偏置；据此设计SAGE框架，融合代数稀疏化（将局部可行候选投影到算子索引代数子空间以抑制虚假分支）和双曲结构引导（将推理状态嵌入负曲率空间提供稠密深度信号）。

Result: 在12个基准和7个模型族上，SAGE优于竞争基线，在开放的Andrews-Curtis长程任务上最高提升8倍。

Conclusion: 结构引导是缓解长程推理偏置的有效设计原则，SCA与SAGE为稀疏奖励下的LLM推理提供了理论与方法框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models (LLMs) under sparse-reward regimes. We argue that this brittleness arises from two biases induced by complex reasoning spaces: an exploration bias, where models are drawn toward locally plausible but structurally unstable branches, and a compounding bias, where small local deviations accumulate across depth and suppress rare rewards. We introduce Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA) as a theoretical lens characterizing how branching structures and sparse rewards induce these biases in long-horizon reasoning with local admissibility, and as a design principle for structural priors in less formal reasoning tasks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose SAGE (Structural Admissibility-Guided Exploration), a unified framework that injects structural guidance to alleviate exploration bias and compounding bias in long-horizon reasoning. SAGE combines two complementary structural guidance: algebraic sparsification, which projects locally admissible candidates onto operator-indexed algebraic subspaces to suppress spurious branching and mitigate exploration bias, and hyperbolic structural guidance, which embeds reasoning states into a negatively curved space to provide dense depth-wise signals and mitigate compounding bias. Across 12 benchmarks and 7 model families, SAGE outperforms competitive baselines. In particular, SAGE achieves up to an 8-fold improvement on the Andrews-Curtis problem, an open real-world long-horizon task. Code is available at: https://github.com/Susan571/SAGE-NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [165] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可扩展框架，自动从纵向EHR笔记生成问答对，构建BRIE基准，经临床医生验证，揭示现有LLM临床助手常遗漏关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM临床助手正融入EHR系统，但其安全性和实用性需严格评估。现有基准多为人工构建、成本高、更新难，随技术发展迅速过时。

Method: 设计可扩展框架，自动从纵向EHR笔记生成问答对；由19名临床医生验证生成器，产出可持续维护的评估数据集BRIE。在9个LLM和5种推理策略上进行评测。

Result: 先进系统常遗漏临床重要信息，尤其在需要跨多文档、多次就诊综合的问答上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 因生成器经过验证，BRIE支持静态基准无法实现的评估：可生成反映临床医生推理差异的多个答案，并持续刷新内容以防数据泄漏。可扩展的基准生成为临床LLM提供严格、最新的评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [IaC-Guard-V: A Verification Framework for LLM-Generated Infrastructure-as-Code Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28488)
*Lokesh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出IaC-Guard-V验证框架，从语法、修复、回归安全和补丁最小性四维评估LLM生成的IaC修复，发现单次修复成功率仅32-50%，验证引导的迭代修复可提升至68-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IaC配置错误是云安全事故主因，LLM被广泛提议为自动修复代理，但其生成修复的可信度尚缺乏研究。IaC验证面临挑战：扫描器行为因工具与配置而异、基础设施语义无法通过普通单元测试验证、供应商特定规则导致验证碎片化。

Method: 提出以验证为中心的框架IaC-Guard-V，从语法有效性、目标问题解决、回归安全、补丁最小性四个维度评估AI生成的IaC修复。构建含70个真实错误配置的Terraform和Kubernetes工件基准，覆盖70条扫描规则和8类违规，用3个LLM族、3种修复策略做630次运行评估。

Result: 所有模型语法有效性达100%，但单次修复仅32-50%通过完整验证；验证引导的迭代修复将验证修复率提升至68-92%。开源模型配验证引导修复以1/12成本超越最强商业模型的非验证修复。商业模型Kubernetes修复接近完美，开源模型需验证引导迭代。结构化提示持续降低Terraform修复质量。

Conclusion: 验证引导对可信IaC修复至关重要，可显著提升修复率并降低成本；结构化提示并非总是有益，挑战了限制LLM输出的常见假设。基准与工件已开源以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) misconfigurations are a leading cause of cloud security incidents, and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as automated repair agents. Yet the trustworthiness of LLM-generated IaC repairs remains underexplored. IaC presents distinctive verification challenges: security scanners may behave differently across tools and configurations, infrastructure semantics cannot be validated through ordinary unit tests, and provider-specific rules create a fragmented verification landscape. We present IaC-Guard-V, a verification-centered framework that evaluates AI-generated IaC repairs through four dimensions: syntactic validity, target-issue resolution, regression safety, and patch minimality. We construct a benchmark of 70 real-world misconfigured Terraform and Kubernetes artifacts spanning 70 unique scanner rules and eight violation classes, and evaluate three repair strategies across three LLM families in 630 runs. Although all models achieve 100% syntactic validity, only 32-50% of single-shot repairs pass full verification. Verification-guided iterative repair significantly improves verified-fix rates to 68-92%. An open-source model with verification-guided repair outperforms the strongest commercial model without verification at one-twelfth the cost per verified fix. Kubernetes repairs reach near-perfect rates for commercial models but require verification-guided iteration for the open-source model. Structured prompting consistently reduces Terraform repair quality, challenging common assumptions about constrained LLM output. The benchmark and artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [167] [Pretraining and adapting a language model on a dependency-free stack: GPT-2 124M from random weights, reproduced against llm.c, and a clinical adapter for Qwen3-0.6B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28568)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 用 Zig 编写的无第三方依赖 ML 栈 numbat 独立实现完整生命周期，预训练 124.4M GPT-2 并做临床适配，结果与参考实现高度一致且吞吐更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几乎所有在役语言模型都只用同一套软件族训练，导致无法区分"关于语言模型的结论"与"关于该软件的结论"。要厘清这一点，需要第二个能跑通完整生命周期（而非仅复现单个算子）的实现。

Method: 使用 numbat（Zig 编写、无第三方运行时依赖的 ML 栈），从随机初始化在 9.91B token 网页文本上预训练 124.4M 参数 GPT-2，再适配一个小模型做临床问答。两阶段均在相同硬件上跑参考实现，并有具停机权限的 sidecar 监督每次运行。

Result: 结果吻合：留出交叉熵 3.2588 vs 已发表 3.29，HellaSwag 0.3053 vs 0.299；8 项配对评测中每点都低于同机参考，平均差 0.0608。参考实现换硬件仅移动 0.0035，界定差距中方法部分的量级。吞吐上 numbat 达 43,374 tok/s，高于 PyTorch 的 41,202，三卡扩展 2.769x；临床适配留出损失 2.1899 vs 2.1941。

Conclusion: 独立实现可复现同一训练生命周期且结果接近、吞吐不劣，说明已知训练结论更多描述模型而非单一软件。两个模型均非医疗器械，未经验证用于临床。

Abstract: Almost every language model in service was trained by one family of software. That concentration makes a question hard to settle: how much of what is known about training a language model describes language models, and how much describes that software? Settling it needs a second implementation able to carry a model through a whole lifecycle rather than reproduce one operator.
  We report such a lifecycle. Using numbat, a machine-learning stack written in Zig with no third-party runtime dependencies, we pretrain a 124.4 M-parameter GPT-2 from random initialisation over 9.91 B tokens of web text, then adapt a separate small model to clinical question answering. A reference implementation runs on identical hardware at both stages, and a sidecar with authority to halt a run supervises each.
  Agreement is close. Held-out cross-entropy finishes at 3.2588 against a published 3.29, and HellaSwag at 0.3053 against 0.299; across 8 paired evaluations it sits below a same-machine reference at every point, by 0.0608 on average. Re-running that reference on different hardware moves it 0.0035, which bounds how much of any gap is method rather than framework. Throughput does not pay for agreement: measured in one session at a production configuration, numbat reaches 43,374 tokens per second against PyTorch's 41,202, scaling 2.769x over three cards. Clinical adaptation ends at 2.1899 held-out loss against 2.1941.
  Neither model is a medical device, and neither is validated for clinical use.

</details>


### [168] [Change-Provenant Supervision: Governing Learned Artifacts Under Policy Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28574)
*Jesus Salas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将"记录血缘"（提出影响范围与依赖解释）与"独立当前契约重验证"（对每个保留目标做可靠性校验）分离；LoRA包与受控修正实验表明该机制能拒绝过期/追加包并保持语义召回，但结论仅限受控治理机制属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录的依赖图无法证明自身无遗漏边，因此对学习产物而言，图范围失效在权威变更后不能单独作为准入依据；当输出测试漏掉一个来源过期的推导时尤其如此。作者希望用独立重验证补足可靠性。

Method: 设计分两层评估。第一层：四个Qwen3-14B LoRA包，将1.028 GB适配器字节绑定到训练与权威记录，比较过期、全新建、朴素追加与血缘选择包。第二层：六项角色分离修正的受控框架，比较传递血缘、文档全局失效与逐修正删边，并配合独立重验证。

Result: 过期与全新建适配器在80个权威中性输入上输出相同计划；字节重建与密封当前契约账本仍拒绝过期和朴素追加包，准入全新建与血缘选择包。受控框架中传递血缘召回40/40语义失效的修正-目标对，同时提名56个影响候选（全局失效为120），56个均有到被修正条款的声明路径，其中16个仍内容有效。每修正删除一个承重边使召回降至34/40，独立重验证拒绝全部六个受影响包。

Conclusion: 结果确立的是受控治理机制的性质，而非企业级失效率、遗忘结果、操作最优流程或测得的总成本降低。

Abstract: A recorded dependency graph cannot certify that it contains no omitted edge. For learned artifacts, graph-scoped invalidation therefore cannot by itself justify admission after authority changes, especially when output testing misses a provenance-stale derivation. We separate recorded lineage, which proposes impact scope and a dependency explanation, from independent current-contract revalidation of every retained target, which supplies soundness relative to the declared contract.
  We evaluate this design in two evidence layers. Four Qwen3-14B LoRA packages bind 1.028 GB of adapter bytes to training and authority records. Stale and full-fresh adapters trained on mutually exclusive versioned supervision emitted identical plans on all 80 tested authority-neutral inputs. Byte-derived reconstruction and a sealed current-contract ledger nevertheless refused the stale and naive-append packages and admitted full-fresh and lineage-selective packages. In a controlled harness with six role-separated amendments, transitive lineage recovered all 40 semantically invalidated amendment-target pairs while nominating 56 impact candidates, compared with 120 under document-wide invalidation. All 56 had a declared path to an amended clause, although 16 remained content-valid. Omitting one load-bearing edge per amendment reduced semantic recall to 34/40 while independent revalidation refused all six affected packages.
  The results establish properties of a controlled governance mechanism, not an enterprise invalidation rate, unlearning result, operationally optimal workflow, or measured total-cost reduction.

</details>


### [169] [Developing a Unified Verification and Validation Activity Standard at JPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28600)
*Maximilian Vierlboeck,Ellen Van Wyk,Natalia Sanchez,Bogdan Oaida,Torrance James Eberhart,Marie Piette Gomez,Christopher Delp,Marijke G. Jorritsma,Alex Desharnais*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JPL通过人本设计研讨开发了统一的V&V活动Schema，基于关系架构区分验证方法并保持通用属性，形式化为SysML模型，并在Jama平台实现，以解决V&V实践碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年JPL的V&V实践出现分化，导致碎片化，增加了管理开销，降低了跨项目效率，并阻碍了机构知识转移，因此需要统一化方案。

Method: 通过涉及29名跨任务类型和学科从业者的人本设计研讨会，开发基于关系架构的统一V&V活动Schema；将方法（测试、分析、检查、演示、设计评审）分离但保持共同属性集；形式化为平台无关的SysML模型，定义需求、V&V活动、场所和证据之间的双向关系。

Result: 在JPL的Jama平台实现，通过模板和模块化项目类型实现受控定制，平衡严谨性与敏捷性，并支持自动化、模式复用和数字线程集成。

Conclusion: 该统一Schema为解决V&V碎片化提供了可行路径，提升跨项目效率与知识转移，兼顾规范性与灵活性。

Abstract: Verification and validation practices (V&V) at NASA's Jet Propulsion Laboratory (JPL) have diverged over the past decade, creating fragmentation that increases overhead, reduces cross-project efficiencies, and inhibits institutional knowledge transfer. We present a unified V&V activity schema developed through human-centered design workshops involving 29 practitioners across multiple mission types and disciplines. The schema builds on a relationship-based architecture that allows for separating methods (Test, Analysis, Inspection, Demonstration, and Review of Design) while maintaining a common attribute set. Formalized as a platform-agnostic SysML model, the schema defines bidirectional relationships between requirements, V&V activities, venues, and evidence. Implementation in JPL's Jama platform demonstrates controlled customization through templates and modular item types, balancing rigor with agility while enabling automations, pattern reuse, and digital thread integration.

</details>


### [170] [Soundness Checking of Taint Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28750)
*Samarth Kishor,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"猜测-验证"方法，用LLM生成方法污点流模型，再用符号算法验证其可靠性，93%的方法模型被证明可靠且引入零新误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态污点流分析依赖用户手写库方法模型，既繁琐又可能不可靠；而通过过程间分析自动建模又效率低下。

Method: (1) LLM agent生成方法的精确污点流模型；(2) 符号算法验证其可靠性：推断模型可靠所必须不发生的污点流，用轻量级静态分析（类型系统、指针分析）证明这些"must-not-flow"，不足时推导最大通用被调方法模型并递归验证，多数情况下避免完整的过程间分析。

Result: 在6个大型Go代码库的97个LLM生成污点流模型上评估，93%被覆盖方法的模型被证明可靠；被证明可靠的模型精度高，证明污点流属性时未产生新的误报。

Conclusion: LLM模型精度直接决定验证效率，精度越高需验证的must-not-flow越少；该方法可有效替代手工建模并避免昂贵的过程间分析。

Abstract: Existing state-of-the-art static taint flow analyses for imperative programming languages can scale to large applications by using precise user-provided taint flow models of library methods. However, manually and precisely modeling a method's taint flows is tedious and potentially unsound. Furthermore, automatically modeling the method via an inter- procedural taint analysis can be inefficient. To solve this problem, we propose a guess-and-check approach: (1) an LLM agent that generates a precise taint flow model of a method and (2) a symbolic algorithm to check the soundness of the model. The algorithm deduces which taint flows must not occur in the method for the LLM's taint flow model to be sound, and uses lightweight static analyses (e.g., type system and pointer analysis) to prove these must-not-flows. When these analyses are insufficient, the algorithm deduces maximally-general callee models and recursively verifies their soundness, avoiding a full inter-procedural taint analysis in most cases. Since a more precise model requires fewer must-not-flows to be verified, the precision of the LLM's model directly determines the efficiency of our approach. We evaluate our approach on 97 LLM-generated taint flow models for methods in 6 large Go codebases and prove the models sound for 93% of the methods they cover. The proven-sound LLM-generated models are also precise, resulting in no new false-positives when proving taint flow properties.

</details>


### [171] [Category-Based MLM: Unifying Powertypes with Superclasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28810)
*Mira Balaban,Azzam Maraee,Arnon Sturm*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CatMLM模型，通过区分category特征与object特征，澄清多层级建模中leveling机制及其与instance-of/subclass关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLM依赖哲学论证，且instance-of关系的非传递语义与深层刻画（deep characterization）相矛盾，缺乏清晰的层级划分标准。

Method: 引入Category-Based MLM（CatMLM），赋予category class双重超类与powertype面向，依据是否参与deep characterization区分category特征与object特征。

Result: 提供了清晰可量化的层级判定标准，以及subclass与instance-of关系的决策规则。

Conclusion: CatMLM给出良定义的MLM模型，阐明层级如何从领域需求中涌现，并分析MLM相较普通OO建模的收益与代价。

Abstract: MultiLevel software Modeling (MLM) suggests that conceptual modeling in broad subject domains might require abstraction of multiple classification levels. The MLM approach relies on philosophical arguments, claiming that faithful modeling of real-world domains involves repeated type classification as in ontologies of natural kinds. MLM leveled architecture is interwoven and defined by instance-of interlevel relationships between clabject classes in lower levels to classes termed category classes, in upper levels. The instance-of relation denotes membership of clabjects as type objects in their (powertypes) category classes, and is not transitive.
  All MLM approaches support forms of deep characterization, i.e., category classes can influence classes in lower levels. Deep characterization is an essential feature of superclasses and contradicts the non-transitive membership meaning of instance-of.
  In this paper, we introduce the Category-Based MLM (CatMLM) model, in which category classes have dual superclass and powertype facets, based on the distinction between category features that do not participate in deep characterization, and object features that do. This distinction clarifies the role of levels, provides a clear quantifiable criterion for leveling, and yields a decision rule between the subclass and instance-of relations.
  The contribution of this paper is to introduce a well-defined MLM model that (1) is based on simple, quantifiable level decisions; (2) clarifies how leveling emerges from domain needs; and (3) analyzes gains and losses of MLM vs. plain OO modeling.

</details>


### [172] [Specification-Driven Benchmarking for Automated Program Repair From Static Corpora to Executable Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28896)
*Yasser Ebrahim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范驱动的基准测试"范式，将APR基准定义为可执行规范，通过生成流水线（生成、验证、语料管理）实现，把基准构建从静态数据集整理转变为声明式实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统APR基准作为静态数据集，其特性继承自所包含的缺陷，存在三大局限：有限语料导致实验控制受限、重复使用易受数据污染、无法随评估需求演化而系统化再生或调整。

Method: 提出规范驱动的基准测试范式：基准由可执行规范定义，规范显式声明目标属性（程序上下文、缺陷分类、难度、验证策略、语料约束），生成流水线通过相互独立的生成、验证和语料管理组件实现这些要求。建立基准规范维度分类法，论证每个维度到确定性架构职责的映射，并提出独立验证是可信基准生成的结构性要求。通过端到端实例展示规范选择如何沿流水线传播并产生可独立验证的基准实例。

Result: 给出该范式的概念基础，包括规范维度分类法、维度到架构职责的映射论证，以及独立验证的结构性必要性的论证；通过端到端示例展示规范选择在流水线中的传播及生成实例的可独立验证性。

Conclusion: 将基准视为可执行规范而非静态数据集，使基准构建从工件整理转向声明式实验设计，为APR基准提供可系统再生、可适应需求演化的实验控制方式。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) benchmarks have traditionally been constructed as static datasets whose characteristics are inherited from the defects they contain. While this paradigm has enabled decades of progress, finite corpora provide limited experimental control, become increasingly susceptible to contamination as they are reused, and cannot be systematically regenerated or adapted as evaluation requirements evolve. We propose specification-driven benchmarking, a paradigm in which benchmarks are defined by executable specifications and realized through benchmark generation. The specification explicitly declares the intended properties of the benchmark (including program context, fault taxonomy, difficulty, validation strategy, and corpus constraints) while a generation pipeline realizes those requirements through independent generation, validation, and corpus management components. We develop the conceptual foundations of this approach by introducing a taxonomy of benchmark specification dimensions, establishing how each specification dimension maps to deterministic architectural responsibilities, and arguing that independent validation is a structural requirement for trustworthy benchmark generation. An end-to-end example illustrates how specification choices propagate through the pipeline to produce benchmark instances whose properties are independently verifiable. By treating the benchmark as an executable specification rather than a static dataset, the proposed paradigm shifts benchmark construction from artifact curation to declarative experimental design.

</details>


### [173] [Human-AI Collaboration for Multi-Line Task Adjustment Using Local Large Language Models and a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29061)
*Teng-Hsien Ko,Chin-Te Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成局部大语言模型、数字孪生与人工决策的多线任务调整系统，采用"提议-验证-决策"工作流，通过30条固定测试记录在四条虚拟手术器械分拣线上评估，结果显示分阶段筛选有效但整体可靠性尚未确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化系统需适应任务、设备状态和人员条件的变化，同时为人工审查提供证据。如何将操作员意图转化为可验证的策略并保留可追溯性，是当前的关键挑战。

Method: 构建多线任务调整系统，集成局部大语言模型、数字孪生和人工决策。采用Propose-Verify-Decide工作流：将操作员意图转化为结构化需求，生成有界候选策略集，并检查语义、仿真执行和操作约束；通过关联记录保留从请求到验证证据与决策的可追溯性。

Result: 30条固定测试记录（28条评估工作流，2条评估模型生成）。18条工作流案例符合预期；自主策略工作流成功率3/10，无效输入正确拒绝率7/8；通过前置检查、产生完整证据并进入最终工程审查（CP6）的4个案例全部通过。8条仿真证据记录的平均放置验证通过率97.50%。首次可审查响应和仿真验证的平均时间（排除启动）分别为12.94秒和164.39秒。遗留失败涉及语义失真、证据不完整和无效输入漏检。

Conclusion: 结果证明了可追溯的策略审查工作流的有效性，但未确立整体可靠性或长期稳定性，需要更广泛的测试和物理评估来评估泛化能力。

Abstract: Automation systems must adapt to changing tasks, equipment states, and staffing conditions while providing evidence for human review. This study presents a multi-line task-adjustment system integrating a local large language model, a digital twin, and human decision-making. A Propose-Verify-Decide workflow translates operator intent into structured requirements, generates a bounded set of candidate strategies, and checks semantics, simulation execution, and operational constraints. Linked records preserve traceability from requests to verification evidence and decisions. Thirty fixed test records were evaluated using four virtual surgical-instrument sorting lines: 28 assessed the workflow and two assessed model generation. Eighteen workflow cases met expectations; autonomous strategy-workflow success was 3/10, and correct rejection of invalid inputs was 7/8. All four cases that passed preceding checks, produced complete evidence, and reached final engineering review (CP6) passed that review. Together with the correct blocking of strategies that failed throughput constraints, this supports the effectiveness of staged screening and confirmation within the tested setting. Mean placement-validation pass rate across eight simulation evidence records was 97.50%. Mean times to the first reviewable response and simulation verification, excluding startup, were 12.94 and 164.39 s, respectively. Remaining failures involved semantic distortion, incomplete evidence, and missed invalid inputs. The results demonstrate a traceable strategy-review workflow, but do not establish overall reliability or long-term stability. Broader testing and physical evaluation are needed to assess generalizability.

</details>


### [174] [Design, development, and preliminary validity and reliability evidence of the Software Engineering Self-Efficacy Scale (SESES)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29068)
*Albert D. Ritzhaupt,Jhonathan Sora-Cardenas,Priya Ganapathy Prasad,Kai A. Hackney,Neha Rani,Pedro Guillermo Feijóo-García*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究设计并初步验证了软件工程自我效能量表（SESES），基于五个维度构建，经527名本科生试点测试与探索性因子分析，证实其五因子多维结构，解释约57%变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程自我效能对本科生学习与发展很重要，但缺乏针对该领域、结合课程指南的专门测量工具。

Method: 基于软件工程课程指南与自我效能理论构建框架，生成87个初始题目，涵盖需求工程、团队协作、软件质量管理、软件设计与架构、敏捷方法五个维度。对527名已完成软件工程课程的本科生进行试点，采用主轴因子法探索性因子分析与Promax斜交旋转检验结构。

Result: EFA得到与理论框架一致的五个月内部一致的潜在因子，交叉负荷极小，模式矩阵结构简单，共同解释约57%的数据变异。

Conclusion: 软件工程自我效能是包含五个相关但独立潜在因子的多维构念；研究讨论了局限与边界，并强调领域特异性测量的必要性。

Abstract: The purpose of this research is to design, develop, implement, and provide preliminary validity and reliability evidence of the Software Engineering Self-Efficacy Scale (SESES). Framed by a conceptual framework using guidance in software engineering curriculum and concepts along with the notion of self-efficacy, we generated an initial item pool of n = 87 items to operationalize and measure software engineering self-efficacy among undergraduate computing students. The conceptual framework traces five dimensions: 1) Requirements Engineering, 2) Teamwork and Collaboration, 3) Software Quality Management, 4) Software Design and Architecture, and 5) Software Agile Methodologies. We pilot tested the SESES with n = 527 undergraduate computing students who had completed a software engineering course in the current semester or a previous academic semester. We employed Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA) with the Principal Axis Factoring method and an oblique (Promax) rotation to examine the underlying structure of the SESES, resulting in the same five internally consistent latent constructs in the conceptual framework with minimal cross-loading and a simple structure in the pattern matrix, explaining approximately 57% of the variability in these data. Our findings suggest that software engineering self-efficacy is a multidimensional construct of five theorized and correlated, yet distinct latent factors. We unpack the limitations and delimitations of the research while exploring undergraduate computing students' software engineering self-efficacy using necessary domain-specific measurements.

</details>


### [175] [VidTutorAssistant: Automating Responses to Programming Tutorial Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29129)
*Ahmad Tayeb,Sonia Haiduc,Mohammad D. Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VidTutorAssistant 是一个自动回答 YouTube 编程教程视频观众问题的 Web 平台，采用检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，在多项评估中取得高准确率，并优于原视频作者的回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YouTube 编程教程的评论区常成为学习者追问的地方，但问题数量远超创作者能回应的范围，导致学习者得不到所需的澄清。

Method: 实现检索增强生成流水线：提取视频字幕并分段、嵌入；将观众评论分类为问题或非问题；对问题通过余弦相似度检索最相关的字幕片段；用 GPT-4 基于检索到的片段作为上下文生成答案。

Result: 在来自 7,522 个 Python 和 Java 教程、105,553 条评论的数据集中抽取 440 条评论进行验证：识别编程语言准确率 0.99；问题/非问题分类准确率 0.96；生成答案正确率 98%、完整率 99.5%，而原创作者回答分别为 89% 和 90%。

Conclusion: VidTutorAssistant 能有效自动识别并回答编程教程视频中的观众问题，其回答在正确性和完整性上均优于原视频作者。

Abstract: Programming tutorial videos on YouTube are an important information resource for software developers and students, and their comment sections have evolved into active spaces where viewers ask follow-up questions. The volume of these questions, however, often exceeds what content creators can address, leaving learners without the clarifications they need. We present VidTutorAssistant, a web platform that automates responses to viewer questions on programming video tutorials. VidTutorAssistant implements a retrieval-augmented generation pipeline that extracts a video's transcript, then segments it and embeds it. It then classifies each viewer comment as being a question or non-question, retrieves the most relevant transcript segments to each identified question via cosine similarity, and then generates an answer to the question using an LLM (GPT-4), while grounding the response using the retrieved transcript segments as context. We validate VidTutorAssistant through a study on a subset of 440 user comments selected from a larger dataset of 105,553 comments extracted from 7,522 Python and Java tutorials. VidTutorAssistant is evaluated on various criteria: a) its ability to identify the programming language in a video, achieving a 0.99 accuracy; b) its ability to classify comments into questions and non-questions, reaching a 0.96 accuracy; and c) its ability to produce correct and complete answers to questions, producing 98% correct and 99.5% complete responses, compared with 89% and 90% for the original creators' answers.

</details>


### [176] [Orchestrating AI-Assisted Code Remediation: Socio-Technical Bottlenecks in a Large Industrial Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29172)
*Andreas Bexell,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma söderberg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过15天工业级单案例田野研究，探究AI辅助大规模代码修复对CI、代码评审和团队协作的影响，发现当编辑成本降低后，CI容量、评审精力和变更编排成为主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型长期代码库的代码退化修复成本高昂，LLM编码助手可规模化执行机械化修复，但其对工业工作流的影响尚缺乏研究。

Method: 在一个闭源工业C++仓库中开展15天探索性单案例田野研究，由经验丰富的开发者使用命令行AI编码助手修复广泛问题；三角验证Gerrit元数据、开发者日志和团队聊天记录，采用描述性统计与定性编码分析。

Result: AI辅助修复快速产生数百次提交、涉及数千行代码，使CI和评审者注意力饱和。朴素的按文件提交压垮了提交即构建CI；改用按目录批量提交并限制每次变更文件数后恢复吞吐量，但仍需显式征求评审、协商可接受的提交粒度并反复跟进解决构建与静态分析失败。

Conclusion: 当机械编辑变得廉价时，CI容量、评审努力和变更编排成为主要瓶颈。大型仓库中可持续的AI辅助修复需要有意识地控制提交、评审和CI批处理粒度，并将语义变更集（如"修复所有warning X"）视为可针对开发者、评审者和CI不同切片的一等工件。

Abstract: Background: Code degradation in large, long-lived codebases is costly to remediate through manual refactoring and opportunistic clean-ups. LLM-based coding assistants can perform mechanical remediation at scale, but their impact on industrial workflows is underexplored. Objective: We investigate how massive AI-assisted code remediation affects build-on-commit continuous integration (CI), code review, and team coordination in a large industrial repository, and which socio-technical bottlenecks constrain such remediation when source editing becomes cheap through AI assistance. Method: We report on a 15-day exploratory single-case field study in which an experienced developer used a command-line AI coding buddy to remediate widespread issues in a closed-source industrial C++ repository. We triangulate Gerrit metadata with a developer diary and team chat, analyzed through descriptive statistics and qualitative coding. Results: AI-assisted remediation rapidly generated hundreds of commits touching thousands of lines, saturating CI and reviewer attention. Naïve per-file commits overloaded build-on-commit CI; Switching to directory-based batching and capping the number of files per change restored throughput, but still required explicit review solicitation, negotiation of acceptable commit granularity, and iterative follow-up to resolve build and static-analysis failures. Conclusion: When mechanical editing is cheap, CI capacity, review effort, and change orchestration become primary bottlenecks. Sustainable AI-assisted remediation in very large repositories requires deliberate control of commit, review, and CI batch granularity and treating semantic change sets, such as ``fix all instances of warning X'', as first-class units of work that can be sliced differently for developers, reviewers, and CI.

</details>


### [177] [HistoRAG: A Citation-Grounded Question Answering Assistant for Teaching with Scanned Local History and Heritage Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29184)
*Hongzhou Duan,Zikun Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HistoRAG，一个面向地方史与文化遗产教学材料的问答助手，能从单一区域馆藏中作答并为每个事实标注卷号与页码。系统用视觉语言模型转录扫描页、保留行级置信度，并构建混合文本索引、关系型目录数据库与知识图谱三种存储，轻量路由器按问题类型分发。在36卷扫描书与同地区遗产目录构成的516题基准上，HistoRAG在准确率上优于段落检索和图检索基线，且每题成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师备课时面对的地方史与文化遗产材料难以使用：主要来源是无文本层的扫描书籍，辅助记录是电子表格形式的行政目录。通用聊天机器人回答流畅但无法提供可验证来源，而来源可追溯性正是教师最需要的属性。

Method: HistoRAG从单一区域馆藏作答：用视觉语言模型逐页转录并基于token概率保留行级置信度；从同一馆藏构建三种存储——混合文本索引、关系型目录数据库、仅从实体密集段落抽取的知识图谱；轻量路由器将问题分派到所需存储，计数类问题进入数据库，关系类问题进入知识图谱。

Result: 在覆盖36卷扫描书籍及同地区遗产目录的516题基准（含统计、事实、时间、多跳问题）上，HistoRAG正确回答数超过段落检索基线和基于图检索的系统，且每题成本远更低。系统运行于聊天界面，教师可对照页码核查任一陈述。

Conclusion: HistoRAG通过多存储检索与行级来源引用，在低成本下实现了可验证的地方史与文化遗产问答，满足教师对来源可信度的核心需求。

Abstract: Teachers who prepare lessons on local history and cultural heritage work from material that is hard to use. The primary sources are scanned books without a text layer, and the supporting records are administrative catalogs released as spreadsheets. A general chatbot answers such questions fluently but without a verifiable source, which is the property a teacher needs most. This paper presents HistoRAG, a question answering assistant that answers from one regional collection and cites a volume and a page for every fact. HistoRAG transcribes each page with a vision language model and keeps a line level confidence from the token probabilities. It builds three stores from the same collection: a hybrid text index, a relational catalog database, and a knowledge graph extracted only from entity dense passages. A lightweight router sends each question to the stores it needs, so that counting questions reach the database and relational questions reach the graph. We build a benchmark of 516 questions over a collection of 36 scanned volumes and the heritage catalogs of the same region, covering statistical, factual, temporal, and multi-hop questions. HistoRAG answers more questions correctly than passage retrieval baselines and than a graph based retrieval system, at a far smaller cost per question. The assistant runs behind a chat interface, so a teacher can check any statement against the page it came from.

</details>


### [178] [On the Impact of Requirement Smells in LLM-Based Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29208)
*Hugo Villamizar,Jannik Fischbach,Mert Şahin,Julian Frattini,Alessio Ferrari,Alexander Korn,Andreas Vogelsang,Daniel Mendez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究需求异味对LLM生成代码功能正确性的影响。通过在清晰需求中逐步引入语义、句法和词汇异味，发现异味密度越高，功能正确性通常越低，且各类异味影响相似。需求质量对代码生成的影响比对其可追溯性的影响更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明需求异味会影响需求与代码间的自动化可追溯性，但其对LLM代码生成的影响缺乏实证证据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于既有可追溯性研究，复用其数据集和需求异味分类法，并扩展至评估LLM生成代码的功能正确性。使用包含四个应用的需求与系统测试的基准，向原本清晰的需求中逐步引入语义、句法和词汇异味，分析其对生成实现的影响。

Result: 异味密度增加通常与基于测试套件的功能正确性下降相关，但无味需求也可能产生错误代码；不同异味类别影响相似。与以往工作相比，需求异味对可追溯性影响温和，而代码生成更敏感。

Conclusion: 需求质量在LLM辅助代码生成中很重要，但仅靠高质量需求并不保证正确性，结果还取决于LLM等因素。研究呼吁进一步探究LLM辅助软件工程中任务相关的质量效应。

Abstract: Software requirements are typically incorporated into prompts used in LLM-assisted software development. Recent work has shown that requirement smells can affect automated traceability between requirements and code, but empirical evidence on their effects in code generation remains limited. To address this gap, we build upon a prior study on automated traceability by reusing its dataset and requirement smell taxonomy, while extending it to evaluate the functional correctness of LLM-generated code. Using a benchmark consisting of requirements and corresponding system tests for four applications, we progressively introduced semantic, syntactic, and lexical smells into otherwise clear requirements and analyzed their influence on generated implementations. Our results suggest that increasing \textit{smell density} was generally associated with lower test-suite-based functional correctness, although non-smelly requirements could still produce faulty code. We also found that different smell categories had similar effects. These findings provide additional empirical evidence of the importance of requirement quality in LLM-assisted code generation, while showing that high-quality requirements alone do not guarantee correctness, as these depends on several factors, including the LLM. Compared with previous work, our results suggest that the impact of requirement smells depends on the software engineering task: whereas their effects on traceability were modest, code generation appears more sensitive. Overall, this work motivates further investigation into task-dependent quality effects in LLM-assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [179] [Model-Based Retargeting to Many-Core CPS: Simulink-to-OpenCL Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29311)
*Ryuga Chiba,Hiroshi Fujimoto,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"工作流保持"的重定向方法，将基于Simulink的CPS应用（含候选级数据并行）移植到OpenCL众核处理器，避免手工重写模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的开发(MBD)与先进众核执行平台之间存在软件可移植性鸿沟，针对新平台手动重写模型代价高。

Method: 利用MathWorks GPU Coder提取数据并行的CUDA代码，再通过自研框架翻译为OpenCL主机端与设备端代码，涵盖语法重写、API仿真及平台特定参数打包。

Result: 在Kalray MPPA Coolidge2上部署了计算密集的Frenet坐标系轨迹规划器，验证了工作流保持的重定向流水线的可行性。

Conclusion: 工作流保持的重定向流程对所评估的CPS工作负载与平台是可行的。

Abstract: This paper addresses the software portability gap between Model-Based Development (MBD) and advanced many-core execution for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS). We present a workflow-preserving retargeting approach for Simulink-based CPS applications with candidate-wise data parallelism to OpenCL-based many-core processors. Rather than manually rewriting models for new platforms, our toolchain uses MathWorks GPU Coder to extract data-parallel CUDA code, which is then translated into OpenCL host and device code via a custom framework. The conversion handles syntax rewriting, API emulation, and platform-specific argument packing. We deployed this workflow for a computationally intensive Frenet-frame trajectory planner on the Kalray MPPA Coolidge2. The results demonstrate the feasibility of a workflow-preserving retargeting pipeline for the evaluated CPS workload and platform.

</details>


### [180] [Demystifying Agent Skills for Smart Contract Auditing: Design, Effectiveness, Behavioral Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29454)
*Cuifeng Gao,Juantao Zhong,Jiachi Chen,Shuai Wang,Daoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统收集83个真实世界的智能合约审计技能，在EVMBench上跨7种agent-model配置评估，发现技能设计轻量但异构，效果主要由模型决定，技能触发是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent（如Claude Code、Codex）已超越编码范畴，可通过"技能"（打包领域知识、工作流与工具指令的可复用产物）增强。但目前对技能如何设计、如何影响agent效果与行为缺乏了解，尤其在智能合约安全审计这一agent表现优异的领域。

Method: 系统收集83个真实智能合约审计技能，在EVMBench上针对7种agent-model配置进行评估，从三个维度分析：(i)技能的设计特征（结构、知识表示、工作流、工具依赖）；(ii)对漏洞检测效果的提升；(iii)对agent执行轨迹的影响。

Result: 审计技能大多轻量但设计异构，覆盖漏洞类型广泛但不均衡。效果主要由模型而非agent框架决定：Codex/GPT-5.5提升最大，检测分数提高22.8%、捕获奖励提高43.2%。技能触发是关键瓶颈；触发后技能保留共享的六阶段审计工作流，但在不同配置下呈现不同的加载模式与差异化行为影响。

Conclusion: 技能效果更多取决于底层模型而非agent框架，技能触发是主要瓶颈。作者发布技能语料与相关产物以支持后续研究。

Abstract: LLM agents, notably Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, are emerging as versatile tools beyond coding agents only. These agents can be enhanced with skills---reusable artifacts that package domain knowledge, workflows, and tool-use instructions. To date, however, little is known about how such skills are designed or how they affect agent effectiveness and behavior in practice. In this paper, we investigate these questions in smart contract security auditing, a domain in which agents have shown substantial promise. We systematically collect 83 smart contract audit skills from the wild and evaluate them on EVMBench across seven agent--model configurations. Our study examines three dimensions: (i) the design characteristics of audit skills, including their structure, knowledge representations, workflows, and tool dependencies; (ii) their effectiveness in improving vulnerability detection; and (iii) their influence on agent execution trajectories. We find that audit skills are mostly lightweight but heterogeneous in design, covering a broad yet imbalanced range of vulnerability types. Their effectiveness is determined primarily by the model rather than the agent harness: Codex/GPT-5.5 achieves the largest gains, improving detection score by 22.8% and captured award by 43.2%. We further find that skill triggering is a key bottleneck. When triggered, skills preserve a shared six-stage audit workflow while exhibiting distinct loading patterns and differential effects on agent behavior across configurations. We release our skill corpus and artifacts to support future research.

</details>


### [181] [Judgment-Centred Software Engineering Education: A Post-Hype Review and Framework for AI-Augmented Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29473)
*Qusay H. Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.SE
